Fine-tuning helps a little, but this version is not accurate enough

The best of three fixed training checkpoints got 12 of 64 new development updates right. Unchanged LoRAcle got 1 of 64. Our success target was at least 70%, so this is a failed development recipe.

Every case was one AdamW update to a fresh rank-16 adapter on the same Qwen3-14B base, trained on four short examples. The interpreter received the released weight-direction tokens, the physical update norm and 33 shuffled choices. It did not receive the training text or the prompts used to measure the effect.

The correct answer was measured: among 32 possible reference formats, cases and orderings on four separate prompts, which gained the most relative likelihood? This is a finite-reference loss preference, not a claim about unrestricted generated behavior. All 64 updates exceeded the fixed effect-size floor.

What went wrong

The selected model predicted lowercase for all 64 actual updates, but 19 measured effects were uppercase. The training targets contained 239 lowercase effects and 17 uppercase effects. Sixteen development cases had composite effect categories that never appeared as measured training targets. The source operations were familiar; the combinations were held out.

The model showed some formatting signal, but its exact score was only four cases ahead of the 8/64 training-majority baseline and seven ahead of its 5/64 shuffled-weight control. The selected-development interval for its advantage over released LoRAcle includes zero at its lower endpoint. Selecting the checkpoint on development data also makes these intervals optimistic for a final claim.

shuffled_ridge0/648/8

Next change

Train one new version to identify detectable change, formatting, case and ordering separately, using class weights computed only from training data. Keep the same combined-answer scoring, examples and meaningful-effect floor. Compare both models on identical new questions. This tests a specific explanation for the failure without turning a partial formatting result into success. The reserved test and fresh confirmation remain unused.
